Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the biotech framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 520. The resulting operator achieves bound O(288/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on biotech structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 520. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 288. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
